{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"call_76e214b609144c5bb47e40d4","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t//! JSON output consistency verification for bf-2nhb.\n2\t//!\n3\t//! These tests lock in the result of auditing every `--format json` code path\n4\t//! across the six commands in scope (list, ready, search, claim, stats,\n5\t//! velocity). The audit found:\n6\t//!\n7\t//! - **Issue-array commands** (`list`, `ready`, `search`, and also `recent`)\n8\t//!   all route through `get_formatter().format_issues()`, emitting JSONL —\n9\t//!   one full `Issue` object per line, NOT a single JSON array. `ready` was\n10\t//!   the last to be migrated (bead bf-64zt).\n11\t//! - **Object commands** (`claim`, `stats`) use the dedicated\n12\t//!   `format_claim_result` / `format_stats` trait methods (a single object,\n13\t//!   never an Issue, never an array) — correct by design.\n14\t//! - **`velocity`** uses `format_velocity` (a JSON array of `VelocityStats`).\n15\t//!\n16\t//! The strongest assertion here is [`issue_array_commands_share_formatter`]:\n17\t//! for the same bead, `list`, `ready`, and `search` emit *byte-identical* JSON.\n18\t//! That can only hold if all three funnel through the shared `JsonFormatter`,\n19\t//! so this test is also the runtime proof that \"no custom `println!` JSON loops\n20\t//! remain\" in the issue-array commands.\n21\t\n22\tuse std::collections::HashMap;\n23\tuse std::path::PathBuf;\n24\tuse std::process::Command;\n25\tuse serde_json::Value;\n26\tuse tempfile::TempDir;\n27\t\n28\t/// Resolve the `bf` binary under test. `CARGO_BIN_EXE_bf` is set automatically\n29\t/// by cargo for integration tests (the package defines a `[[bin]] name = \"bf\"`).\n30\tfn bf_path() -> PathBuf {\n31\t    std::env::var(\"CARGO_BIN_EXE_bf\")\n32\t        .map(PathBuf::from)\n33\t        .unwrap_or_else(|_| PathBuf::from(\"./target/debug/bf\"))\n34\t}\n35\t\n36\t/// Run `bf` with `--workspace <ws>` and the given args; assert success and\n37\t/// return stdout as a String.\n38\tfn run(ws: &std::path::Path, args: &[&str]) -> String {\n39\t    let out = Command::new(bf_path())\n40\t        .arg(\"--workspace\")\n41\t        .arg(ws)\n42\t        .args(args)\n43\t        .output()\n44\t        .unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!(\"failed to spawn bf {:?}: {e}\", args));\n45\t    assert!(\n46\t        out.status.success(),\n47\t        \"bf {:?} failed (exit {:?}): {}\",\n48\t        args,\n49\t        out.status.code(),\n50\t        String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr)\n51\t    );\n52\t    String::from_utf8(out.stdout).expect(\"bf stdout is not valid UTF-8\")\n53\t}\n54\t\n55\tfn init_workspace() -> TempDir {\n56\t    let dir = TempDir::new().unwrap();\n57\t    run(dir.path(), &[\"init\", \"--prefix\", \"test\"]);\n58\t    dir\n59\t}\n60\t\n61\t/// Create a bead; returns its printed id.\n62\tfn create_bead(ws: &std::path::Path, title: &str, extra: &[&str]) -> String {\n63\t    let mut args = vec![\"create\", \"--title\", title, \"--type\", \"task\", \"--priority\", \"2\"];\n64\t    args.extend_from_slice(extra);\n65\t    run(ws, &args).trim().to_string()\n66\t}\n67\t\n68\t/// Parse JSONL (one Issue per line) into a map keyed by bead id. Lines that are\n69\t/// empty or the literal `[]` (ready's empty placeholder) are skipped.\n70\tfn jsonl_by_id(stdout: &str) -> HashMap<String, Value> {\n71\t    let mut map = HashMap::new();\n72\t    for line in stdout.lines() {\n73\t        let line = line.trim();\n74\t        if line.is_empty() || line == \"[]\" {\n75\t            continue;\n76\t        }\n77\t        let v: Value = serde_json::from_str(line)\n78\t            .unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!(\"invalid JSON line {line:?}: {e}\"));\n79\t        if let Some(id) = v.get(\"id\").and_then(|i| i.as_str()) {\n80\t            map.insert(id.to_string(), v);\n81\t        }\n82\t    }\n83\t    map\n84\t}\n85\t\n86\t// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n87\t// Issue-array commands: list / ready / search\n88\t// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n89\t\n90\t/// The cornerstone consistency test. For the same bead, `list`, `ready`, and\n91\t/// `search` must emit byte-identical JSON — only achievable if all three share\n92\t/// `get_formatter().format_issues()`. Any custom `println!` loop in one of them\n93\t/// would diverge (e.g. a different field set or ordering) and fail here.\n94\t#[test]\n95\tfn issue_array_commands_share_formatter() {\n96\t    let ws = init_workspace();\n97\t    let assigned = create_bead(\n98\t        ws.path(),\n99\t        \"shared formatter probe\",\n100\t        &[\"--assignee\", \"worker-9\", \"--label\", \"phase-1\"],\n101\t    );\n102\t    let _other = create_bead(ws.path(), \"another bead\", &[]);\n103\t\n104\t    let list_out = run(ws.path(), &[\"list\", \"--format\", \"json\"]);\n105\t    let ready_out = run(ws.path(), &[\"ready\", \"--limit\", \"0\", \"--format\", \"json\"]);\n106\t    let search_out = run(ws.path(), &[\"search\", \"--format\", \"json\"]);\n107\t\n108\t    let from_list = list_out\n109\t        .lines()\n110\t        .find(|l| l.contains(&format!(\"\\\"id\\\":\\\"{assigned}\\\"\")))\n111\t        .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!(\"bead {assigned} missing from list output\"));\n112\t    let from_ready = ready_out\n113\t        .lines()\n114\t        .find(|l| l.contains(&format!(\"\\\"id\\\":\\\"{assigned}\\\"\")))\n115\t        .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!(\"bead {assigned} missing from ready output\"));\n116\t    let from_search = search_out\n117\t        .lines()\n118\t        .find(|l| l.contains(&format!(\"\\\"id\\\":\\\"{assigned}\\\"\")))\n119\t        .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!(\"bead {assigned} missing from search output\"));\n120\t\n121\t    assert_eq!(\n122\t        from_list, from_ready,\n123\t        \"list and ready diverged — they do not share the formatter\"\n124\t    );\n125\t    assert_eq!(\n126\t        from_list, from_search,\n127\t        \"list and search diverged — they do not share the formatter\"\n128\t    );\n129\t}\n130\t\n131\t/// `list`/`ready`/`search` emit JSONL (one JSON object per line), not a single\n132\t/// JSON array. Every line is a parseable object that always carries `assignee`\n133\t/// (null when unset) and `labels` (always an array) — the bf-1wj display contract.\n134\t#[test]\n135\tfn issue_arrays_are_jsonl_with_stable_fields() {\n136\t    let ws = init_workspace();\n137\t    create_bead(ws.path(), \"bare bead\", &[]);\n138\t    create_bead(\n139\t        ws.path(),\n140\t        \"labeled bead\",\n141\t        &[\"--assignee\", \"worker-1\", \"--label\", \"x\"],\n142\t    );\n143\t\n144\t    for cmd in &[\"list\", \"ready\"] {\n145\t        let out = run(\n146\t            ws.path(),\n147\t            &[cmd, \"--limit\", \"0\", \"--format\", \"json\"],\n148\t        );\n149\t        // Not a wrapped array: the whole stdout is not itself a JSON array.\n150\t        let trimmed = out.trim();\n151\t        assert!(\n152\t            !trimmed.starts_with('['),\n153\t            \"{cmd} output must be JSONL, not a JSON array\"\n154\t        );\n155\t        assert!(\n156\t            !trimmed.is_empty(),\n157\t            \"{cmd} should have >0 beads to inspect\"\n158\t        );\n159\t        for line in out.lines() {\n160\t            let v: Value = serde_json::from_str(line).expect(\"each JSONL line is valid JSON\");\n161\t            assert!(v.is_object(), \"{cmd} line is not an object: {line}\");\n162\t            assert!(v.get(\"id\").is_and_some(), \"{cmd} missing id\");\n163\t            assert!(v.get(\"title\").is_and_some(), \"{cmd} missing title\");\n164\t            assert!(v.get(\"status\").is_and_some(), \"{cmd} missing status\");\n165\t            assert!(\n166\t                v.get(\"assignee\").is_some(),\n167\t                \"{cmd} must always emit assignee (bf-1wj)\"\n168\t            );\n169\t            assert!(\n170\t                v.get(\"labels\").map_or(false, |l| l.is_array()),\n171\t                \"{cmd} labels must be an array (bf-1wj)\"\n172\t            );\n173\t        }\n174\t    }\n175\t}\n176\t\n177\t/// The `--json` shorthand must be identical to `--format json`.\n178\t#[test]\n179\tfn json_alias_matches_format_flag() {\n180\t    let ws = init_workspace();\n181\t    create_bead(ws.path(), \"alias probe\", &[]);\n182\t    let via_format = run(ws.path(), &[\"list\", \"--format\", \"json\"]);\n183\t    let via_alias = run(ws.path(), &[\"list\", \"--json\"]);\n184\t    assert_eq!(via_format, via_alias, \"--json must equal --format json\");\n185\t}\n186\t\n187\t/// `search` for a known term returns only matching beads as JSONL.\n188\t#[test]\n189\tfn search_jsonl_is_consistent_with_list() {\n190\t    let ws = init_workspace();\n191\t    let needle = create_bead(ws.path(), \"unique-needle-title\", &[]);\n192\t    let _hay = create_bead(ws.path(), \"unrelated-bead\", &[]);\n193\t\n194\t    let search_map = jsonl_by_id(&run(ws.path(), &[\"search\", \"unique-needle\", \"--format\", \"json\"]));\n195\t    let list_map = jsonl_by_id(&run(ws.path(), &[\"list\", \"--format\", \"json\"]));\n196\t\n197\t    // search returned exactly the needle.\n198\t    assert_eq!(search_map.len(), 1, \"search should return only the needle\");\n199\t    // and that bead's JSON is identical to what list emits for it.\n200\t    assert_eq!(\n201\t        search_map.get(&needle),\n202\t        list_map.get(&needle),\n203\t        \"search and list disagree on the same bead's JSON\"\n204\t    );\n205\t}\n206\t\n207\t// assert_eq!(v.get(\"assignee\").and_then(|a| a.as_str()), Some(\"claimer-1\"));\n223\t    // Not an Issue object (no status/priority/issue_type keys) and not an array.\n224\t    assert!(\n225\t claim_dry_run_emits_preview_object() {\n235\t    let ws = init_workspace();\n236\t    create_bead(ws.path(), \"claimable\", &[]);\n237\t\n238\t    let out = run(\n239\t        ws.path(),\n240\t        &[\"claim\", \"--assignee\", \"previewer\", \"--dry-run\", \"--format\", \"json\"],\n241\t    );\n242\t    let v: Value = serde_json::from_str(out.trim()).expect(\"dry-run output is one JSON JSON output consistency verification for bf-2nhb.\n//!\n//! These tests lock in the result of auditing every `--format json` code path\n//! across the six commands in scope (list, ready, search, claim, stats,\n//! velocity). The audit found:\n//!\n//! - **Issue-array commands** (`list`, `ready`, `search`, and also `recent`)\n//!   all route through `get_formatter().format_issues()`, emitting JSONL —\n//!   one full `Issue` object per line, NOT a single JSON array. `ready` was\n//!   the last to be migrated (bead bf-64zt).\n//! - **Object commands** (`claim`, `stats`) use the dedicated\n//!   `format_claim_result` / `format_stats` trait methods (a single object,\n//!   never an Issue, never an array) — correct by design.\n//! - **`velocity`** uses `format_velocity` (a JSON array of `VelocityStats`).\n//!\n//! The strongest assertion here is [`issue_array_commands_share_formatter`]:\n//! for the same bead, `list`, `ready`, and `search` emit *byte-identical* JSON.\n//! That can only hold if all three funnel through the shared `JsonFormatter`,\n//! so this test is also the runtime proof that \"no custom `println!` JSON loops\n//! remain\" in the issue-array commands.\n\nuse std::collections::HashMap;\nuse std::path::PathBuf;\nuse std::process::Command;\nuse serde_json::Value;\nuse tempfile::TempDir;\n\n/// Resolve the `bf` binary under test. `CARGO_BIN_EXE_bf` is set automatically\n/// by cargo for integration tests (the package defines a `[[bin]] name = \"bf\"`).\nfn bf_path() -> PathBuf {\n    std::env::var(\"CARGO_BIN_EXE_bf\")\n        .map(PathBuf::from)\n        .unwrap_or_else(|_| PathBuf::from(\"./target/debug/bf\"))\n}\n\n/// Run `bf` with `--workspace <ws>` and the given args; assert success and\n/// return stdout as a String.\nfn run(ws: &std::path::Path, args: &[&str]) -> String {\n    let out = Command::new(bf_path())\n        .arg(\"--workspace\")\n        .arg(ws)\n        .args(args)\n        .output()\n        .unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!(\"failed to spawn bf {:?}: {e}\", args));\n    assert!(\n        out.status.success(),\n        \"bf {:?} failed (exit {:?}): {}\",\n        args,\n        out.status.code(),\n        String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr)\n    );\n    String::from_utf8(out.stdout).expect(\"bf stdout is not valid UTF-8\")\n}\n\nfn init_workspace() -> TempDir {\n    let dir = TempDir::new().unwrap();\n    run(dir.path(), &[\"init\", \"--prefix\", \"test\"]);\n    dir\n}\n\n/// Create a bead; returns its printed id.\nfn create_bead(ws: &std::path::Path, title: &str, extra: &[&str]) -> String {\n    let mut args = vec![\"create\", \"--title\", title, \"--type\", \"task\", \"--priority\", \"2\"];\n    args.extend_from_slice(extra);\n    run(ws, &args).trim().to_string()\n}\n\n/// Parse JSONL (one Issue per line) into a map keyed by bead id. Lines that are\n/// empty or the literal `[]` (ready's empty placeholder) are skipped.\nfn jsonl_by_id(stdout: &str) -> HashMap<String, Value> {\n    let mut map = HashMap::new();\n    for line in stdout.lines() {\n        let line = line.trim();\n        if line.is_empty() || line == \"[]\" {\n            continue;\n        }\n        let v: Value = serde_json::from_str(line)\n            .unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!(\"invalid JSON line {line:?}: {e}\"));\n        if let Some(id) = v.get(\"id\").and_then(|i| i.as_str()) {\n            map.insert(id.to_string(), v);\n        }\n    }\n    map\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Issue-array commands: list / ready / search\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n/// The cornerstone consistency test. For the same bead, `list`, `ready`, and\n/// `search` must emit byte-identical JSON — only achievable if all three share\n/// `get_formatter().format_issues()`. Any custom `println!` loop in one of them\n/// would diverge (e.g. a different field set or ordering) and fail here.\n#[test]\nfn issue_array_commands_share_formatter() {\n    let ws = init_workspace();\n    let assigned = create_bead(\n        ws.path(),\n        \"shared formatter probe\",\n        &[\"--assignee\", \"worker-9\", \"--label\", \"phase-1\"],\n    );\n    let _other = create_bead(ws.path(), \"another bead\", &[]);\n\n    let list_out = run(ws.path(), &[\"list\", \"--format\", \"json\"]);\n    let ready_out = run(ws.path(), &[\"ready\", \"--limit\", \"0\", \"--format\", \"json\"]);\n    let search_out = run(ws.path(), &[\"search\", \"--format\", \"json\"]);\n\n    let from_list = list_out\n        .lines()\n        .find(|l| l.contains(&format!(\"\\\"id\\\":\\\"{assigned}\\\"\")))\n        .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!(\"bead {assigned} missing from list output\"));\n    let from_ready = ready_out\n        .lines()\n        .find(|l| l.contains(&format!(\"\\\"id\\\":\\\"{assigned}\\\"\")))\n        .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!(\"bead {assigned} missing from ready output\"));\n    let from_search = search_out\n        .lines()\n        .find(|l| l.contains(&format!(\"\\\"id\\\":\\\"{assigned}\\\"\")))\n        .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!(\"bead {assigned} missing from search output\"));\n\n    assert_eq!(\n        from_list, from_ready,\n        \"list and ready diverged — they do not share the formatter\"\n    );\n    assert_eq!(\n        from_list, from_search,\n        \"list and search diverged — they do not share the formatter\"\n    );\n}\n\n/// `list`/`ready`/`search` emit JSONL (one JSON object per line), not a single\n/// JSON array. &[\"--assignee\", \"worker-1\", \"--label\", \"x\"],\n    );\n\n    for cmd in &[\"list\", \"ready\"] {\n        let out = run(\n            ws.path(),\n            &[cmd, \"--limit\", \"0\", \"--format\", \"json\"],\n        );\n        // Not a wrapped array: the whole stdout is not itself a JSON array.\n        let trimmed = out.trim();\n        assert!(\n assert!(v.get(\"status\").is_and_some(), \"{cmd} missing status\");\n            assert!(\n                v.get(\"assignee\").is_some(),\n                \"{cmd} must always emit assignee (bf-1wj)\"\n            );\n            assert!(\n                v.get(\"labels\").map_or(false, |l| l.is_array()),\n                \"{cmd} labels must be an array (bf-1wj)\"\n            );\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// The `--json` shorthand must be identical to `--format json`.\n#[test]\nfn json_alias_matches_format_flag() {\n    let ws = init_workspace();\n    create_bead(ws.path(), \"alias probe\", &[]);\n    let via_format = run(ws.path(), &[\"list\", \"--format\", \"json\"]);\n    let via_alias = run(ws.path(), &[\"list\", \"--json\"]);\n    assert_eq!(via_format, via_alias, \"--json crate::format::{ClaimResultOutput, Formatter, StatsOutput, JsonEnvelope};\nuse crate::model::Issue;\nuse crate::velocity::VelocityStats;\nuse serde_json::{self, Map, Value};\n\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]\npub struct JsonFormatter;\n\nimpl JsonFormatter {\n    /// Create a JsonFormatter with envelope mode enabled.\n    pub fn with_envelope_enabled() -> Self {\n        JsonFormatter\n    }\n}\n\n/// Serialize a single issue to a JSON object, stripping the bulky\n/// dependencies/comments relations for `br` compatibility, and guaranteeing\n/// that `assignee` and `labels` are always present.\n///\n/// The `Issue` struct skips `assignee` when `None` and `labels` when empty so\n/// the on-disk JSONL stays compact and `bd`-compatible. That is the wrong shape\n/// for CLI consumers of `ready`/`list`/`search --format json`: a downstream\n/// filter that deserializes into a struct with optional `assignee`/`labels`\n/// fields cannot tell an omitted key from a genuinely unset value. We therefore\n/// normalize the display output so `assignee` is always emitted (`null` when\n/// unset) and `labels` is always an array (`[]` when empty).\nfn issue_to_value(issue: &Issue) -> Value {\n    let mut stripped = issue.clone();\n    stripped.dependencies = vec![];\n    stripped.comments = vec![];\n\n    let mut value = serde_json::to_value(&stripped).unwrap_or(Value::Null);\n    if let Value::Object(ref mut map) = value {\n        ensure_display_fields(map);\n    }\n    value\n}\n\n/// Guarantee the `assignee` and `labels` keys exist on a serialized issue map.\nfn ensure_display_fields(map: &mut Map<String, Value>) {\n    map.entry(\"assignee\")\n        .or_insert(Value::Null);\n    map.entry(\"labels\")\n        .or_insert_with(|| Value::Array(vec![]));\n}\n\nimpl Formatter for JsonFormatter {\n    fn format_issue(&self, issue: &Issue) -> String {\n        serde_json::to_string(&issue_to_value(issue)).unwrap_or_else(|_| \"{}\".to_string())\n    }\n\n    fn format_issues(&self, issues: &[Issue]) -> String {\n        issues\n            .iter()\n            .map(|issue| serde_json::to_string(&issue_to_value(issue)))\n            .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()\n            .unwrap_or_default()\n            .join(\"\\n\")\n    }\n\n    fn format_error(&self, message: &str) -> String {\n        serde_json::json!({\"error\": message}).to_string()\n    }\n\n    fn format_claim_result(&self, result: &ClaimResultOutput) -> String {\n        serde_json::to_string(result).unwrap_or_else(|_| \"{}\".to_string())\n    }\n\n    fn format_no_claim(&self) -> String {\n        \"{}\".to_string()\n    }\n\n    fn format_stats(&self, stats: &StatsOutput) -> String {\n        serde_json::to_string(stats).unwrap_or_else(|_| \"{}\".to_string())\n    }\n\n    fn format_velocity(&self, stats: &[VelocityStats]) -> String {\n        serde_json::to_string(stats).unwrap_or_else(|_| \"[]\".to_string())\n    }\n\n    fn format_with_envelope(&self, kind: &str, data: &str) -> String {\n        // Parse the data string as JSON\n        let json_value: Value = serde_json::from_str(data)\n            .unwrap_or_else(|_| Value::String(data.to_string()));\n\n        // Wrap in envelope and serialize\n        JsonEnvelope::new(kind, json_value)\n            .to_json_compact()\n            .unwrap_or_else(|_| \"{}\".to_string())\n    }\n\n    fn format_with_envelope_and_warning(&self, kind: &str, data: &str, warning: Option<&str>) -> String {\n        // Parse the data string as JSON\n        let json_value: Value = serde_json::from_str(data)\n            .unwrap_or_else(|_| Value::String(data.to_string()));\n\n        // Wrap in envelope with optional warning and serialize\n        let envelope = JsonEnvelope::new(kind, json_value);\n        let envelope_with_warning = match warning {\n            Some(w) => envelope.with_warning(w),\n            None => envelope,\n        };\n        envelope_with_warning\n            .to_json_compact()\n            .unwrap_or_else(|_| \"{}\".to_string())\n    }\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use crate::model::Issue;\n    use serde_json::Value;\n\n    fn parse(line: &str) -> Value {\n
